# FORMABLE NATIONS DUAL SCRIPTED GUIS

# Formable Nations Reworked - Scripted GUIs for Toggle Buttons

# Initialize/Toggle Debug Window
FormableNationsReworkedDebug_initialize = {
	scope = country
	saved_scopes = { }
	is_shown = { always = yes }
	is_valid = { always = yes }
	effect = {
		# Called when opening the settings window: refresh the enabled-formables count
		fnr_count_fnr_formables_effect = { VAR = fnr_active_fnr_formables }
		fnr_count_vanilla_formables_effect = { VAR = fnr_active_vanilla_formables }
		set_variable = { name = fnr_active_formables value = var:fnr_active_fnr_formables }
		change_variable = { name = fnr_active_formables add = var:fnr_active_vanilla_formables }
		fnr_count_formables_by_category_effect = yes
	}
}

# Toggle for Historical Formables - fnr_historical_enabled
	# Existed after EU5 start date and not after January 1, 1837
	FormableNationReworked_toggle_historical_formables = {
		scope = country
		is_shown = { always = yes }
		is_valid = { always = yes }
		effect = {
			# Check player's current state
			if = {
				limit = { has_variable = fnr_historical_enabled }
				# Currently enabled, so disable for ALL countries
				every_country = {
					remove_variable = fnr_historical_enabled
				}
			}
			else = {
				# Currently disabled, so enable for ALL countries
				every_country = {
					set_variable = {
						name = fnr_historical_enabled
						value = yes
					}
				}
			}
		}
	}

# Toggle for Non-Historical Formables - fnr_nonhistorical_enabled
	# Existed before EU5 start date
	FormableNationReworked_toggle_nonhistorical_formables = {

				every_country = {
					set_variable = {
						name = fnr_nonhistorical_enabled
						value = yes
					}
				}
			}
		}
	}

# Toggle for Papal States Formables - fnr_is_papacy
	# For Papal States
	FormableNationReworked_toggle_papal_formables = {
		scope = country
		saved_scopes = { }
		is_shown = { always = yes }
		is_valid = { always = yes }
		effect = {
			if = {
				limit = { has_variable = fnr_is_papacy }
				# Currently enabled, so disable for ALL countries
				every_country = {
					limit = {
						has_reform = government_reform:papacy_reform
						tag = PAP
					}
					remove_variable = fnr_is_papacy
				}
			}
			else = {
				# Currently disabled, so enable for ALL countries
				every_country = {
					limit = {
						has_reform = government_reform:papacy_reform
						tag = PAP
					}
					set_variable = fnr_is_papacy
				}
			}
		}
	}	


# Individual Formable Toggle List
	# Rank 1 
		# Pamiristan
			FormableNationReworked_toggle_pamiristan = {
				scope = country
				is_shown = { always = yes }
				is_valid = { always = yes }
				effect = {
					if = { # Cycle: Default → Force OFF → Force ON → Default
						limit = {
							NOT = { has_variable = fnr_pamiristan_enabled }
							NOT = { has_variable = fnr_pamiristan_disabled }
						}
						every_country = { # Currently DEFAULT → Force OFF
							set_variable = { name = fnr_pamiristan_disabled value = yes }
						}
					}
					else_if = { # Currently FORCE OFF → Force ON
						limit = { has_variable = fnr_pamiristan_disabled }
						every_country = { 
							remove_variable = fnr_pamiristan_disabled
							set_variable = { name = fnr_pamiristan_enabled value = yes }
						}
					}
					else_if = { # Currently FORCE ON → Default
						limit = { has_variable = fnr_pamiristan_enabled }
						every_country = {
							remove_variable = fnr_pamiristan_enabled
						}
					}
				}
			}

							NOT = { has_variable = fnr_bactria_enabled }
							NOT = { has_variable = fnr_bactria_disabled }
						}
						every_country = { # Currently DEFAULT → Force OFF
							set_variable = { name = fnr_bactria_disabled value = yes }
						}
					}
					else_if = { # Currently FORCE OFF → Force ON
						limit = { has_variable = fnr_bactria_disabled }
						every_country = { 
							remove_variable = fnr_bactria_disabled
							set_variable = { name = fnr_bactria_enabled value = yes }
						}
					}
					else_if = { # Currently FORCE ON → Default
						limit = { has_variable = fnr_bactria_enabled }
						every_country = {
							remove_variable = fnr_bactria_enabled
						}
					}
				}
			}

				scope = country
				is_shown = { always = yes }
				is_valid = { always = yes }
				effect = {
					if = { # Cycle: Default → Force OFF → Force ON → Default
						limit = {
							NOT = { has_variable = fnr_czechoslovakia_enabled }
							NOT = { has_variable = fnr_czechoslovakia_disabled }
						}
						every_country = { # Currently DEFAULT → Force OFF
							set_variable = { name = fnr_czechoslovakia_disabled value = yes }
						}
					}
					else_if = { # Currently FORCE OFF → Force ON
						limit = { has_variable = fnr_czechoslovakia_disabled }
						every_country = { 
							remove_variable = fnr_czechoslovakia_disabled
							set_variable = { name = fnr_czechoslovakia_enabled value = yes }
						}
					}
					else_if = { # Currently FORCE ON → Default
						limit = { has_variable = fnr_czechoslovakia_enabled }
						every_country = {
							remove_variable = fnr_czechoslovakia_enabled
						}
					}
				}
			}

					if = { # Cycle: Default → Force OFF → Force ON → Default
						limit = {
							NOT = { has_variable = fnr_tengoku_enabled }
							NOT = { has_variable = fnr_tengoku_disabled }
						}
						every_country = { # Currently DEFAULT → Force OFF
							set_variable = { name = fnr_tengoku_disabled value = yes }
						}
					}
					else_if = { # Currently FORCE OFF → Force ON
						limit = { has_variable = fnr_tengoku_disabled }
						every_country = { 
							remove_variable = fnr_tengoku_disabled
							set_variable = { name = fnr_tengoku_enabled value = yes }
						}
					}
					else_if = { # Currently FORCE ON → Default
						limit = { has_variable = fnr_tengoku_enabled }
						every_country = {
							remove_variable = fnr_tengoku_enabled
						}

				is_shown = { always = yes }
				is_valid = { always = yes }
				effect = {
					if = { # Cycle: Default → Force OFF → Force ON → Default
						limit = {
							NOT = { has_variable = fnr_tarraconensis_enabled }
							NOT = { has_variable = fnr_tarraconensis_disabled }
						}
						every_country = { # Currently DEFAULT → Force OFF
							set_variable = { name = fnr_tarraconensis_disabled value = yes }
						}
					}
					else_if = { # Currently FORCE OFF → Force ON
						limit = { has_variable = fnr_tarraconensis_disabled }
						every_country = {
							remove_variable = fnr_tarraconensis_disabled
							set_variable = { name = fnr_tarraconensis_enabled value = yes }
						}
					}
					else_if = { # Currently FORCE ON → Default
						limit = { has_variable = fnr_tarraconensis_enabled }
						every_country = {
							remove_variable = fnr_tarraconensis_enabled
						}
					}
				}
			}

					else_if = { # Currently FORCE ON → Default
						limit = { has_variable = fnr_michigan_enabled }
						every_country = {
							remove_variable = fnr_michigan_enabled
						}
					}
				}
			}

# Toggle for Vanilla Formables - fnr_vanilla_formables_disabled (inverted: set means OFF, so vanilla
# formables stay on in existing saves and new games without any setup)
	FormableNationReworked_toggle_vanilla_formables = {
		scope = country
		is_shown = { always = yes }
		is_valid = { always = yes }
		effect = {
			if = {
				limit = { has_variable = fnr_vanilla_formables_disabled }
				every_country = {
					remove_variable = fnr_vanilla_formables_disabled
				}
			}
			else = {
				every_country = {
					set_variable = { name = fnr_vanilla_formables_disabled value = yes }
				}
			}
		}
	}

						every_country = {
							set_variable = { name = fnr_vanilla_sweden_disabled value = yes }
						}
					}
					else_if = {
						limit = { has_variable = fnr_vanilla_sweden_disabled }
						every_country = {
							remove_variable = fnr_vanilla_sweden_disabled
							set_variable = { name = fnr_vanilla_sweden_enabled value = yes }
						}
					}
					else_if = {
						limit = { has_variable = fnr_vanilla_sweden_enabled }
						every_country = {
							remove_variable = fnr_vanilla_sweden_enabled
						}
					}
				}
			}

		# vanilla_spa
			FormableNationReworked_toggle_vanilla_spa = {
				scope = country
				is_shown = { always = yes }
				is_valid = { always = yes }
				effect = {
					if = { # Cycle: Default -> Force OFF -> Force ON -> Default
						limit = {
							NOT = { has_variable = fnr_vanilla_spa_enabled }
							NOT = { has_variable = fnr_vanilla_spa_disabled }
						}
						every_country = {
							set_variable = { name = fnr_vanilla_spa_disabled value = yes }
						}
					}
					else_if = {
						limit = { has_variable = fnr_vanilla_spa_disabled }
						every_country = {
							remove_variable = fnr_vanilla_spa_disabled
							set_variable = { name = fnr_vanilla_spa_enabled value = yes }
						}
					}
					else_if = {
						limit = { has_variable = fnr_vanilla_spa_enabled }
						every_country = {
							remove_variable = fnr_vanilla_spa_enabled
						}
					}
				}
			}

						limit = { has_variable = fnr_vanilla_liv_disabled }
						every_country = {
							remove_variable = fnr_vanilla_liv_disabled
							set_variable = { name = fnr_vanilla_liv_enabled value = yes }
						}
					}
					else_if = {
						limit = { has_variable = fnr_vanilla_liv_enabled }
						every_country = {
							remove_variable = fnr_vanilla_liv_enabled
						}
					}
				}
			}

						every_country = {

						}
					}

							NOT = { has_variable = fnr_vanilla_hau_disabled }
						}
						every_country = {
							set_variable = { name = fnr_vanilla_hau_disabled value = yes }
						}
					}
					else_if = {
						limit = { has_variable = fnr_vanilla_hau_disabled }
						every_country = {
							remove_variable = fnr_vanilla_hau_disabled
							set_variable = { name = fnr_vanilla_hau_enabled value = yes }
						}
					}
					else_if = {
						limit = { has_variable = fnr_vanilla_hau_enabled }
						every_country = {
							remove_variable = fnr_vanilla_hau_enabled
						}
					}

						limit = {
							NOT = { has_variable = fnr_vanilla_ryu_enabled }
							NOT = { has_variable = fnr_vanilla_ryu_disabled }
						}
						every_country = {
							set_variable = { name = fnr_vanilla_ryu_disabled value = yes }
						}
					}
					else_if = {
						limit = { has_variable = fnr_vanilla_ryu_disabled }
						every_country = {
							remove_variable = fnr_vanilla_ryu_disabled
							set_variable = { name = fnr_vanilla_ryu_enabled value = yes }
						}
					}
					else_if = {
						limit = { has_variable = fnr_vanilla_ryu_enabled }
						every_country = {
							remove_variable = fnr_vanilla_ryu_enabled
						}
					}
				}
			}

					if = { # Cycle: Default -> Force OFF -> Force ON -> Default
						limit = {
							NOT = { has_variable = fnr_vanilla_mor_enabled }
							NOT = { has_variable = fnr_vanilla_mor_disabled }
						}
						every_country = {
							set_variable = { name = fnr_vanilla_mor_disabled value = yes }
						}
					}
					else_if = {
						limit = { has_variable = fnr_vanilla_mor_disabled }
						every_country = {
							remove_variable = fnr_vanilla_mor_disabled
							set_variable = { name = fnr_vanilla_mor_enabled value = yes }
						}
					}
					else_if = {
						limit = { has_variable = fnr_vanilla_mor_enabled }
						every_country = {
							remove_variable = fnr_vanilla_mor_enabled
						}
					}
				}
			}

# Reset every individual formable toggle to Default (the category decides)
	FormableNationReworked_reset_individual_toggles = {
		scope = country
		is_shown = { always = yes }
		is_valid = { always = yes }
		effect = {
			every_country = {
				if = { limit = { has_variable = fnr_abbasid_enabled } remove_variable = fnr_abbasid_enabled }
				if = { limit = { has_variable = fnr_abbasid_disabled } remove_variable = fnr_abbasid_disabled }
				if = { limit = { has_variable = fnr_abyssinia_enabled } remove_variable = fnr_abyssinia_enabled }
				if = { limit = { has_variable = fnr_abyssinia_disabled } remove_variable = fnr_abyssinia_disabled }
				if = { limit = { has_variable = fnr_adriaticempire_enabled } remove_variable = fnr_adriaticempire_enabled }
				if = { limit = { has_variable = fnr_adriaticempire_disabled } remove_variable = fnr_adriaticempire_disabled }
				if = { limit = { has_variable = fnr_afghanistan_enabled } remove_variable = fnr_afghanistan_enabled }
				if = { limit = { has_variable = fnr_afghanistan_disabled } remove_variable = fnr_afghanistan_disabled }
				if = { limit = { has_variable = fnr_alabamaus_enabled } remove_variable = fnr_alabamaus_enabled }
				if = { limit = { has_variable = fnr_alabamaus_disabled } remove_variable = fnr_alabamaus_disabled }
				if = { limit = { has_variable = fnr_alaska_enabled } remove_variable = fnr_alaska_enabled }

# Counts the formables the current settings allow, on the player (fnr_active_fnr_formables, fnr_active_vanilla_formables)
	FormableNationReworked_count_active = {
		scope = country
		is_shown = { always = yes }
		is_valid = { always = yes }
		effect = {
			fnr_count_fnr_formables_effect = { VAR = fnr_active_fnr_formables }
			fnr_count_vanilla_formables_effect = { VAR = fnr_active_vanilla_formables }
			set_variable = { name = fnr_active_formables value = var:fnr_active_fnr_formables }
			change_variable = { name = fnr_active_formables add = var:fnr_active_vanilla_formables }
			fnr_count_formables_by_category_effect = yes
		}
	}


# Presets (settings window): set every category at once. Individual toggles and the Papal States toggle are left alone.
	# Default - Historical and Vanilla, as at game start
	FormableNationReworked_preset_default = {
		scope = country
		is_shown = { always = yes }
		is_valid = { always = yes }
		effect = {
			every_country = {
				set_variable = { name = fnr_historical_enabled value = yes }
				if = { limit = { has_variable = fnr_nonhistorical_enabled } remove_variable = fnr_nonhistorical_enabled }
				if = { limit = { has_variable = fnr_althistory_enabled } remove_variable = fnr_althistory_enabled }
				if = { limit = { has_variable = fnr_modern_enabled } remove_variable = fnr_modern_enabled }
				if = { limit = { has_variable = fnr_controversial_enabled } remove_variable = fnr_controversial_enabled }
				if = { limit = { has_variable = fnr_fantasy_enabled } remove_variable = fnr_fantasy_enabled }
				if = { limit = { has_variable = fnr_meme_enabled } remove_variable = fnr_meme_enabled }
				if = { limit = { has_variable = fnr_us_states_enabled } remove_variable = fnr_us_states_enabled }
				if = { limit = { has_variable = fnr_vanilla_formables_disabled } remove_variable = fnr_vanilla_formables_disabled }
			}
		}
	}

	# Plausible - Historical, Non-Historical, Alt-History and Vanilla
	FormableNationReworked_preset_plausible = {
		scope = country
		is_shown = { always = yes }
		is_valid = { always = yes }
		effect = {
			every_country = {
				set_variable = { name = fnr_historical_enabled value = yes }
				set_variable = { name = fnr_nonhistorical_enabled value = yes }
				set_variable = { name = fnr_althistory_enabled value = yes }
				if = { limit = { has_variable = fnr_modern_enabled } remove_variable = fnr_modern_enabled }
				if = { limit = { has_variable = fnr_controversial_enabled } remove_variable = fnr_controversial_enabled }
				if = { limit = { has_variable = fnr_fantasy_enabled } remove_variable = fnr_fantasy_enabled }
				if = { limit = { has_variable = fnr_meme_enabled } remove_variable = fnr_meme_enabled }
				if = { limit = { has_variable = fnr_us_states_enabled } remove_variable = fnr_us_states_enabled }
				if = { limit = { has_variable = fnr_vanilla_formables_disabled } remove_variable = fnr_vanilla_formables_disabled }
			}
		}
	}

	# Everything - every category on
	FormableNationReworked_preset_everything = {
		scope = country
		is_shown = { always = yes }
		is_valid = { always = yes }
		effect = {
			every_country = {
				set_variable = { name = fnr_historical_enabled value = yes }
				set_variable = { name = fnr_nonhistorical_enabled value = yes }
				set_variable = { name = fnr_althistory_enabled value = yes }
				set_variable = { name = fnr_modern_enabled value = yes }
				set_variable = { name = fnr_controversial_enabled value = yes }
				set_variable = { name = fnr_fantasy_enabled value = yes }
				set_variable = { name = fnr_meme_enabled value = yes }
				set_variable = { name = fnr_us_states_enabled value = yes }
				if = { limit = { has_variable = fnr_vanilla_formables_disabled } remove_variable = fnr_vanilla_formables_disabled }
			}
		}
	}

	# Vanilla Only - only the base game's formables
	FormableNationReworked_preset_vanilla_only = {
		scope = country
		is_shown = { always = yes }
		is_valid = { always = yes }
		effect = {
			every_country = {
				if = { limit = { has_variable = fnr_historical_enabled } remove_variable = fnr_historical_enabled }
				if = { limit = { has_variable = fnr_nonhistorical_enabled } remove_variable = fnr_nonhistorical_enabled }
				if = { limit = { has_variable = fnr_althistory_enabled } remove_variable = fnr_althistory_enabled }
				if = { limit = { has_variable = fnr_modern_enabled } remove_variable = fnr_modern_enabled }
				if = { limit = { has_variable = fnr_controversial_enabled } remove_variable = fnr_controversial_enabled }
				if = { limit = { has_variable = fnr_fantasy_enabled } remove_variable = fnr_fantasy_enabled }
				if = { limit = { has_variable = fnr_meme_enabled } remove_variable = fnr_meme_enabled }
				if = { limit = { has_variable = fnr_us_states_enabled } remove_variable = fnr_us_states_enabled }
				if = { limit = { has_variable = fnr_vanilla_formables_disabled } remove_variable = fnr_vanilla_formables_disabled }
			}
		}
	}
